# Free and Open Source (FOSS) Hytale Mods

A curated and automatically updated directory of **Free and Open Source (FOSS)** mods, plugins, and tools for [Hytale](https://hytale.com) hosted on [CurseForge](https://www.curseforge.com/hytale).

[![FOSS Mods](https://img.shields.io/badge/FOSS%20Mods-933-brightgreen)](#categories) [![Total Scanned](https://img.shields.io/badge/CurseForge%20Projects-6338-blue)](https://www.curseforge.com/hytale) [![Total Downloads](https://img.shields.io/badge/Total%20Downloads-11,448,762-orange)](#) [![Last Updated](https://img.shields.io/badge/Last%20Updated-2026-09-11%2009:55%20UTC-informational)](#)

> **Note**: A mod is included here if the author has provided a verified public source repository (GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, etc.) in their CurseForge project settings. Support the open source modding community by starring and contributing to their repositories!

## 🔄 Updating this List

This list is generated automatically using `generate_readme.py`. To refresh the list:

```bash
# Run with default settings (uses Python 3 standard library only, no external dependencies)
python3 generate_readme.py

# Optional: specify custom output file or export structured JSON
python3 generate_readme.py --output README.md --json-output mods.json
```

## 💡 How to Add Your Mod

If you are a Hytale mod author on CurseForge and want your mod to appear in this list:
1. Go to your project on CurseForge and click **Edit Project**.
2. Add your GitHub/GitLab/Codeberg repository link into the **Source Code** URL field.
3. Once saved, run the updater script or wait for the next automated update!
